# Feature status: the spec against the code, end to end

Every requirement of `BUILD_SPEC.md` and `docs/design/installer-ui.md`, one row each, with what the code does about it: **built** (the code does what the line says; evidence is `file:line`), **partial** (exists, but a stated part is missing or differs; the "missing" cell says exactly what), **missing** (no code), **spec_dropped** (superseded by a decision in spec section 13 or `docs/HANDOFF.md`). Made 2026-11-09 on `main` at 733f943 (0.1.12) by eight `sonnet` readers, one per spec area, each re-checked by an `opus` judge that opened every cited line and added the rows the reader skipped (workflow `feature-status-audit`; raw result in `D:\NightShift-refs\feature-audit-2026-11-09.json`). Line numbers drift; names are what to search for. The "verified" column is the evidence level of `docs/VERIFICATION.md` where those documents say so.

Every partial and missing row is a box in section H at the end; `dev/finalization_status.py` counts them with the boxes of `docs/FINALIZATION.md`. Done means section H is empty of open boxes.

## Decisions the operator took where the spec contradicts itself (2026-11-09)

The judges found places where two spec lines disagree and the code follows one of them. The code's behaviour is kept and the spec gets a section 13 row (a box in section H):

- Free disk: 60 GB for Small, 74 GB Balanced, 81 GB Best (spec 1.2 and the installer), not the ~40 GB of A.2.
- Stock-footage keys: optional, at least one recommended; setup finishes without any (Pexels stopped issuing keys).
- Git: not installed by setup; `ns update --git` is the optional developer path and says so (NS-E702 hint).
- Default home: the largest free SSD (spec 5 and 21.5), not `%LOCALAPPDATA%` (1.3).
- Driver: CUDA 12.4 or newer with the 12.4 build; an RTX 50-series card takes the 13.x build (spec 1.2 rule), the 21.1 line is updated.
- faster-whisper on the GPU: a failed CUDA probe falls back to the CPU with NS-W404 instead of stopping setup.

## Installer, install sources, uninstall, update (spec A, 5, 21, J1, J8)

Judge's notes: I checked the cited code for every row whose evidence was in doubt, and spot-checked the rest (welcome.txt, copy.ts, answers_view.py, s01_questions.py, s03, verify.py tts_modes, preflight.py, stage1.ps1 disk/network/winget rows, bootstrap.py TOOLS, cli.py, update.py git_update, Settings.tsx, svc_settings.py, uninstall.ps1, make_bundle.ps1, doctor.py, plan.py, models_get.py, fallback.py, manifest.json, build-qwentts.yml). Changes: (1) The "Install Git" row moves from spec_dropped to partial. The drop is recorded only in a code comment (bootstrap.py:35-36) and HANDOFF #62. A.3 step 2, 5 Stage 1, 21.1 and J1 step 3 all still require Git, and section 13 has no decision row for it. (2) "J1 step 3" moves from built to partial for the same reason (Git is listed but never installed). (3) The "1.2 Network" row moves from built to partial: an unreachable Hugging Face, PyTorch, Pexels or Pixabay only gives a warning row, but the spec says NS-E105. (4) The "s08 faster-whisper" row moves from built to partial: on failure setup continues on the CPU (NS-W404) and the spec section was never updated. (5) The "J1 step 1" row is corrected: make_bundle.ps1:240-244 does copy answers.example.json to the zip root. I added that -WebView2Dir puts 3 binary DLLs into bin/webview2, which breaks the 'no binaries' rule in 1.3. (6) The A.5 voice row now notes that the dashboard checks clip length (ns_tts/sample.py:19-20, at least 3 s, cut at 30 s, not 10 s). The installer checks nothing. (7) The "re-register shortcuts" row: the update swaps <home>\app in place, so existing .lnk targets stay valid. The real gap is that new or changed shortcuts in a release never appear, and the proof was rewritten to match. (8) The driver row notes the conflict inside the spec: 21.1 says CUDA 12.8 or newer, 1.2 says 12.4. (9) Added rows: bundle contents (no uv.lock); ns doctor --bundle contents; app_path recorded; qwentts GitHub Actions build; no disk check for two-model mode (+20 GB) or the PyTorch fallback (+10 GB); OPERATOR.md and CLAUDE.md in the bundle. A.4 and A.6 (pipeline outputs) belong to the studio, clipper and dashboard chunks and are not audited here. Five places where the spec contradicts itself need an owner decision before code can be 'green': A.2 ~40 GB vs 1.2 60 GB; 1.3 default home %LOCALAPPDATA% vs 5/21.5 best drive; 21.1 CUDA 12.8 vs 1.2 12.4; stock keys Required vs optional in code; Git required vs dropped.

## Runtime, supervisor, services, launchers, CLI, logging, errors (spec 6, 15.3-15.5, 15.9-15.13, J9, J10)

Judge's notes: I re-checked the cited code against BUILD_SPEC sections 6, 6.1, 6.2, 15.3-15.5, 15.9-15.13, J9 and J10. Most reader rows hold up. Confirmed by reading: core.py start/health/crash/ensure/swap/acquire/shutdown, app.py (routes, lifespan cleanup, the 300/600 s health timeout from health_cfg), the launchers (dashboard, code, studio, update), schedule.py (WakeToRun, StartWhenAvailable, 6 h limit, S4U then interactive fallback), s07 opencode.json (matches 15.10 exactly), s09 (seven shortcuts), backup TREES, the errors.json codes and how often each is referenced (all E6xx codes from the 18.8 table exist and are referenced), the tts server routes, embed_client, the studio and ns CLI command lists, and update._reregister. Changes: (1) dev\run_services_dev.ps1: built -> partial. Its embed window leaves out the -c/-b/-ub flags the supervisor adds through embed_server_args, so any input over 512 tokens gets HTTP 500. It never passes -t. Its -NsHome parameter is not exported as NIGHTSHIFT_HOME, so ns_tts.server (Paths.from_env) can read a different home. (2) Warm-up strip (still partial): added that the '(9 GB)' size can never show while a model loads. vram_mb is only recorded after the service is ready, and the server never sets engine.vram_gb that Shell.tsx reads. (3) J9 'closing the terminal releases the GPU': verified V-ci -> U. The gap was found by reading code (opencode is not in LEASED_PREFIXES, and there is no close handler); no test proves it. (4) Owners row: says plainly that the 'gate' owner has no caller because ns gate was removed. (5) Added the J10 Language row (UI in English; story language set per channel through ns_common/languages.py). Still missing or partial after re-check: studio dashboard and ns gate commands (no code, and the spec was not struck); the module-level embed_client.embed(); prompt_versions is never read by learning; /events never carries kind 'job' (only the dashboard's own hub publishes job frames); there is no close handler on the Engine console; the health timeout default is 300/600 s, not 120; voice files are speaker.spk/.rvq, not speaker.bin; the NS-W301 text is stale; NS-E301 has no retry; calibrate --score needs --channel; --github --channel main is refused; ns update does not re-register shortcuts or show the changelog; supervisor_client.py is not in ns_common; the owner never appears in the status strip.

## Studio pipeline: master-first, Shorts, formats, TTS chunking, captions, QC, SEO, publishing (spec 7, 9, 10, 15.2a, 15.8, J2, J3)

Judge's notes: I re-read every cited file:line in D:\NightShift-main. Changes: (1) Master-first row: the stage count was wrong. MASTER_FIRST_STAGES has 19 names, install_short_combos adds short_combos and the policy gates add 2 more, so the plan has 22 stages, not 21. (2) 'wpm adjusted by measured voice speed' went from partial to missing. Nothing measures a voice's wpm. pacing_speed only scales the TTS speed. (3) M4 went from built to partial. pairwise_continuity() is true only for cpu-dev or ctx <= 8192, but the 16 GB profile (gpu, llm_tier small) runs at 16K (spec section 3, ns_supervisor/tune.py:53). So the 16 GB profile never runs continuity on chapter pairs. (4) The 14/30-day reuse row went from built to partial. When the pool is used up, select_for_beat calls pick_for_slots with blocked=set() and warns NS-W405, which breaks the cross-video windows. (5) New row: the final-gate ClipReuse check (NS-E613) reads this job's clip_usage rows, but those rows are written by record_output in the outbox stage, which runs after policy_final. On a normal run the check sees zero rows and always passes. The tests only pass because they insert rows by hand. (6) New row: chapter text added by the duration_check expansion skips the text policy gate. run_stage calls refresh_upstream_hashes for story.json, so policy_text is marked done on the expanded story and never re-checks it. (7) Hook row: the shorts_only fallback hook is the part's whole first sentence with no 12-word cut (stages_shorts.py:292). The master path does cut it. The spec's own 1.5 s limit cannot be met by 12 words. (8) Fixed evidence: the Stage Protocol and run_stage are at ns_studio/stages.py:18-28 and :61-89, not :158-229. test_safe_slug_is_windows_safe is in tests/test_clipper_stages.py, not test_outbox.py. (9) Added notes to rows I kept as built: - shorts_per_master has no 4-6 validator. - M1 dedupes only against the channel's last 50 ideas. - A Short's caption drift reuses the master's 'long' figure. - Shorts of a series part get '(Part n)' in the title. - The Shorts Searcher can repeat landscape Pexels searches the master already made. - series_compilation evidence now cites spec lines 338 and 459. Every other built row matched its code.

| Status | Feature | Spec | Evidence | Missing / note | Proof | Verified |

Needed: after voice, compute words / audio seconds per voice from audio/manifest.json and story word counts; store it (e.g. a voices table column or config/voice_wpm.json); have master_budget/short_budget use it instead of PACING_WPM[pacing]. The only measured value today is the TTS real-time factor for timeouts (ns_common/tts_timeout.py). | After two jobs, a per-voice wpm is stored and the second job's plan_meta.json wpm differs from the table value | U |

## Variation, prompts, rubric, validators, calibration, presets, safety and growth playbook, governor, learning (spec 8, 16, 17, 18, J5)

Judge's notes: Judge pass over the reader's rows. I opened the cited code (grep and read only), checked that all 55 cited test names exist under tests/, and compared everything against BUILD_SPEC sections 8, 16-18 and J5. Downgraded from built to partial: (1) "{hook, structure} pair window". draw.py:166-169 drops the rule for derived Shorts once every hook with the master's structure is in the window. With the default 4 hooks and 4-6 Shorts per master, Shorts 4-6 always repeat a pair. (2) "Formats are data". The story renderers ignore the format presets: media/render.py:33-34 hard-codes 1080x1920 and 1920x1080, and only clipper/ and series/ load presets/formats. (3) "Channels list formats_allowed". ensure_allowed is called only by clipper/stages_clip.py and series/workshop.py:941. `studio run` and `--daily` story jobs never check it. (4) "Warn below 4x4". The Dashboard warns at fewer than 3 options and has no 4x4 rule. (5) "Weeks 3-6". Publish times use shorts_per_day_max, not the plan (daily.py:113, outbox.py:153), so "stay at 1/day" never reaches the schedule. (6) Caption emphasis. prompts/clip_select.md never asks for `emphasis`, so clips with a real model get no key word, although the field and wiring exist. (7) Caption text source. clip_select does not ask the model to restore casing and punctuation, and "like" is deliberately kept as a filler (clipper/select.py:22). Upgraded: the evidence for calibration step 4 now notes that two-model mode does exist in ns_supervisor/services.py:46-77. It switches on whenever llama.story_model_path is set, but calibration never sets it. Evidence fixed: - policy/judge.py is 91 lines, so the cited :229-286 became :34-84. - learn/reweight.py is 143 lines, so the citations became :20, :34-48 and :81-115. - REAL_PLACES holds about 37 names, not 45. - J5 step 3 now cites ns_dashboard/svc_engine.py:201-204. "Tonight's plan" is static text there ("Makes X at run_time") and never calls the governor. - The Dashboard pause buttons (Channels.tsx:31, Queue.tsx:356) post no note at all, against J7's "Pause with a note". This was added to the incident row. - Master jobs' story.json has chapters and no `text` (models.py:468), which confirms that `review show` prints no story for masters. Verified level: mask_profanity, caption styles, the learn status row and formats_allowed were raised to V-ci because tests exist. Rows added: 18.10 bullet 2 (owner-written editorial fields), the "no title cards, no fades" part of 18.5 (folded into the hook row), and the 18.12 "otherwise re-run calibration" advice (folded into the milestone row). Confirmed as accurate: the partial rows for the NS-E304 three-idea loop, opening similarity at 0.9 and skipped in dev, reading level not configurable, cliff variety checked by embedding instead of by device, the critic receiving no exemplars, age restriction never true (PolicyCheckResult has no intensity field and the judge defaults it to "low"), no final-gate safety re-check, spectral flatness absent, the Anton font never downloaded, no incident procedure in the README, and the Dashboard skipping pool minimums, distinctness and the 3-channel cap.

## Nightshift Clipper (spec 19, 23.5, J4)

Judge's notes: Judge pass over all 85 reader rows. I re-read BUILD_SPEC 19.1-19.7, 23.5, J4 and 17.2, opened the cited code, and checked that every named test function exists in tests/ (all of them do; CI runs pytest -m "not network" in .github/workflows/ci.yml). Changes: (1) "C7 render stage holds an NVENC tenant" moved from partial to built. BUILD_SPEC section 0 rule 8 and the Stage contract (line 616) define the tenants as llama/tts/whisper/None only. "NVENC" in the 19.2 table names the encoder, not a lease, and the story-video M13 render (media/render.py:301) is also tenant None. (2) "C6 optional filler removal" moved from built to partial: 17.2 says um/uh/like, but select.py:21-23 HESITATIONS leaves "like" out on purpose and no decision records this. (3) "23.5 step 1 Analyze" evidence fixed: analyze.py is only 167 lines, so the cited 158-235/238-266 do not exist. The real places are analyze.py:71-81, 84-95, 98-126 and 135-167. Same fix for the analyze.py:297-307 citation in the 23.5 step 3 row (now 157-167). (4) Emphasis row made sharper: select.Cand, to_cand and _item (select.py:44-57, 134-160, 384-401) never carry `emphasis`, so a model emphasis would be lost even if the prompt asked for it. CR-C6-6 in contracts/CHANGE_REQUESTS.md lists this as still open. (5) Facecam-area row now cites CR-C8-7, which proposes 5 % and is not marked applied; BUILD_SPEC still says 8 %. (6) GET /api/templates row: the templates.py docstring promises "Pillow previews", but there is no preview code or route. CR-C6-6 also lists "templates.py still reads clip_templates.yaml" as open. (7) The verified field on missing rows is set to unknown. For the 19.6 transcription-speed row, ns_supervisor/verify.py:118-140 only loads whisper and never times it. Confirmed after checking: the C3 visual speaker refinement is missing (no stage rewrites transcript speakers), the stacked border has no larger-face fallback (layout.py:283-296 and detect.py speaking_from_motion return None below 0.5), title_card has no reader anywhere in ns_studio, video_kind is never set (models.py:85, stages_clip.py:352), and judge and render are staged, not done per clip. Added rows: C3/C4/C8 tenants, podcast_single needs (it was implicit), the YuNet download path, and a silent face-skip risk. Risks not given rows: (a) 17.2 says stacked podcast layouts put captions on the seam, but 19.3 says lower third; the code follows 19.3 (anchor_y_pct 75). The spec contradicts itself, so the owner should pick one. (b) Stable faces are counted once over the whole video (detect_video), so a guest who is on screen for less than 70 % of the video never counts, and that clip gets talking_head, not podcast_stacked. That is allowed by 23.5 but worth an owner decision. (c) ClipParams allows spacing_s down to 0 and max_clips up to 20. The API caps max_clips at 10 and never sets spacing, so the default of 60 s holds.

## Dashboard pages, API, job worker (spec 20.1-20.5, J2, J6, J7)

Judge's notes: Judge pass over the Dashboard chunk (BUILD_SPEC 20.1-20.5, J2, J6, J7). I opened the cited code for every row I changed and for the main built rows (Queue.tsx, Create.tsx, Channels.tsx, Results.tsx, Settings.tsx, Library.tsx, Shell.tsx, svc_engine.py, jobs.py, svc_outputs.py, svc_channels.py, svc_library.py, assistant/tools.py, ns_studio/cli.py, ns_common/venv_extras.py, templates.py, the tests). What changed and why: (1) Queue checklist after Approve: confirmed from the code. It is no longer a guess. The Ready tab lists status=pending only (Queue.tsx:24). The list reloads when the outbox counts change (Queue.tsx:45). Status is polled every 10 s and also pushed over SSE (lib/app.tsx:72-87). So an approved item leaves the Ready list and the Review falls back to items[0]. 'Finish posting' on the Approved tab sets tab=ready and current=id (Queue.tsx:88). That id is not in the pending list, so it also opens a different item or the empty state. Steps 2-11 cannot be reached for an approved video. Undo only clears a local flag (Queue.tsx:285). The row stays partial with the exact gap. J2.4 is updated to match. (2) `studio ask` upgraded partial -> built. The studio venv gets the supervisor extra, which has fastapi (ns_common/venv_extras.py:14-17, 21-23), so mount_ask mounts the real command. tests/test_cli.py:307 covers it. (3) J6.3 clone sample upgraded partial -> built. 3-30 s accepts every 3-10 s sample. (4) VoiceDesign row: the missing text is corrected. `ns models get` now exists (ns_supervisor/cli.py:396), so the 501 only shows with an old ns. The real gaps are: nothing downloads on first use (design fails with NS-E314, svc_voices.py:88), and progress is a button label plus a status line polled every 4 s, with no bar or percent. (5) Verified levels corrected. Non-loopback/Origin -> V-ci (tests exist). Assistant busy-queue -> V-ci (test exists). Assistant chat V-gpu -> V-dev: the inventory records only the dev journey, and a real Qwen run is not recorded. Fixed a typo in the media test name (616 -> 416). (6) 1366x768 row: added concrete evidence. The Results drop zone is the last item on the page (Results.tsx:98), so with data it is below the fold. (7) The Assistant never-registered row now also cites uninstall, settings import/export and engine flags. set_schedule passes only schedule_time and notifications (tools.py:109-114). Rows added: the Assistant operating tools list (spec names search_library; the code has search_stories, search_footage and find instead), confirm cards via POST /api/assistant/confirm {action_id}, transcripts in data/assistant.db, the versioned behaviour prompt, J2.3 'reject feeds the critic bar' (built through the rejection rate in cadence; the reason text itself is not used), and J7 'README/Operator doc lists the review checklist (18.11)'. That last one is missing: no strike or incident checklist exists in README.md or OPERATOR.md. Confirmed unchanged by reading the code: no autoplay; formats_allowed has no editor control (only 4 SECTIONS in Channels.tsx:91); Pause sends no note; Results merges top and bottom into one list and shows no unmatched list; the job card shows 'Working' while it waits for the GPU (_stage_of returns None); the status pill prints the raw 'voice (10/16)' and has no Done state; Create and Library cancel have no confirm; GET /templates has no PNGs; the story excerpt is 1500 characters; policy notes read 'OK: <name>'.

## Assistant and Series Workshop (spec 20.3a, 23)

Judge's notes: Judge pass over 20.3a and 23.1-23.4, read-only in D:\NightShift-main. I opened every cited file:line and grepped for the cited tests. All the cited tests exist, though test_help_lists_every_command exists in both tests/test_series_tools.py:129 and tests/test_ns_cli.py. Status changes: (1) status.yaml row, built -> partial. Workshop.mark_published (workshop.py:1036-1041) has no caller in ns_studio or ns_dashboard, and no tool or CLI exposes it. Only tests call it. Marking a video uploaded (svc_outputs.py:238-252) never moves its part to 'published', so a part stops at 'produced' and the story never reaches 'published'. (2) Standalone-Shorts row, built -> partial. 23.4 says the closing line points "to the part". The short_hook.md prompt and DEFAULT_CLOSINGS point to "the full story on the channel" and never name Part N. New rows: (a) design.yaml settings are applied in production -> partial. caption_style and visual_treatment are applied (variation/stages.py:57-58), and so are voice (media/voice.py:25), thumbnail_style (media/thumbnails.py:200) and cadence. But design.format is never read: produce always uses fmt='master_first' (workshop.py:989, studio_bridge.py run_series_part). (b) The orientation tools cannot see workshop stories. FOLDERS has no 'stories', and find/where_is do not search <home>\stories. Proof and verification corrections: - find, where_is, explain_error, describe_tool, list_tools and search_docs are all called directly in tests/test_dashboard_assistant.py::test_orientation_tools_through_the_registry. find goes from U to V-ci, and the where_is proof now cites that test. - studio ask: the loop is proven by tests/test_dashboard_library.py::test_ask_cli_runs_the_same_loop and ::test_ask_cli_reports_busy_engine_and_errors. tests/test_cli.py only checks that the command is mounted. - search_docs: the reason for dropping BUILD_SPEC.md and ENGINEERING.md is also written in the code comment at docs_index.py:19-22, not only in CHANGELOG. The row stays partial. Re-checked and confirmed: - The three missing items really are absent: embed rerank, design preview frame, and the AVD > 40% unlock. Grep finds no rerank, preview or avd logic in ns_studio/series or ns_dashboard/assistant. The analytics.avg_pct_viewed column does exist, so the unlock is buildable. - Other partials verified exactly: chapter floor of 5 (workshop.py:85-87,131-133), cadence index-only spacing (workshop.py:1003), title has no "of M" (seo.py:163-166 long, 95-96 short), notes.md hand comments not read (revise_part takes only the feedback argument), static_map lacks services/channels/formats (orientation.py:206-210), fill_from_context matches by exact arg name (loop.py:46-62; UI sends story_slug while workshop tools take slug), and the devfake planner has no workshop or orientation phrasings (devfake.py:159-203; 'draft 3 story concepts' matches no rule). - Engine flags stay unreachable: set_schedule whitelists schedule_time and notifications.

## Setup window (docs/design/installer-ui.md)

Judge's notes: Judge pass over the reader rows against docs/design/installer-ui.md (sections 0-6) and the code in D:\NightShift-main. I opened the cited code for every row that looked risky. Most reader rows held up; the changes are below. Confirmed in code, kept as the reader had them: the hhmm() bug (server sends 'HH:MM' or 'HH:MM:SS', format.ts:26-31 calls new Date() on it, so the Checks, Failed and Installing times render empty); the declined-UAC message being returned with ok=true (controller.py:1018-1029 plus useSetup.tsx toasting only when !ok); stage1's 60 s ping wait; HOST_WAIT_S=20; the ref12 ports missing units/readOnly/vessel/danger; installer/ui having no package.json; hf_only_blocked being called only from tests; no 'uninstall' fixture; no CI step running -CheckOnly. Downgraded: (1) Done admin note, built to partial. controller.py:1809-1813 adds an admin warning that carries USERNAME, and Done.tsx:24 adds its own generic copy.adminNote, so an admin run shows the note twice, and the UI copy does not name the user. (2) Host message protocol, built to partial. CloseGraceMs=1500 (nightshift-host.ps1:42), the spec says 3 s. (3) Close behaviour 6.5, built to partial and verified V-dev to U. The body copy says 'Nightshift setup window' and the spec says 'black setup window'. VERIFICATION.md section G lists the WebView2 bridge close confirm as U, and the dialog can only be reached inside the host. (4) The connection banner, the Download header and the Stock footage keys title, built to partial, because only their copy differs from the spec. The spec was never amended (grep finds 'black setup window' only in the spec). (5) Design review and partner acceptance, built to partial and verified to U. Nothing records an owner review at 1366x768 and 1024x640 in both themes, or a partner run. (6) U6 host on the partner PC and DPI scaling, verified changed to U. VERIFICATION.md:125 lists the WebView2 bridge as U. Upgraded or re-classed: (7) Edge case '-Ui with an invalid answers.json', missing to partial. controller.py:862-872 refuses Start with 'answers.json had problems. Fix them on the Choices screen.' What is still missing: the marked fields and the footer line. (8) Edge case 'keys rejected: Install disabled until both keys work', partial to spec_dropped. Section 2.3's validation table, which is newer, makes both keys optional, and the code follows 2.3. Cleaned up: built rows that carried a 'missing' note which is not a gap (the Copy test report amendment, the Time left card, CI ui-build, pexelsKey re-check, the check-key Pexels path) now have the note moved into evidence. Added 7 rows the reader skipped: - Welcome 'Open log folder' fails before answers exist: Welcome.tsx builds meters.disk.path\logs, which machine.revealable does not allow, so the server answers 409 'That folder cannot be opened from here'. - Browser app-mode arguments. - Progress events at most 4 per second. - Download groups with active rows pinned to the top. - Winget rows' 'Windows may ask for permission' note. - Verify and Done footers. - Installing header description copy (differs from the spec).

## H. Feature gaps (one box per partial or missing row; tick only with `evidence:`)

Each line names the area, the feature, what is missing, how to prove it, and the spec reference. The builders' workflow takes them in order; the laptop proofs follow `docs/FINALIZATION.md` rules.
